---

## Task 1.6: the deprecation set and `permissions:report-dead`

**The deprecation set is 22 keys, authoritative** (§4.9.3), every one spelled in its **post-rename** form, and **every one of the 22 was verified present in the 325-key catalogue** before this revision shipped:

`audit.view`, **`catalog-cart.convert_so`**, **`catalog-cart.manage_all`**, `inventory.receive`, `invoices.print`, `menus.view`, `pos.issue_goodwill_voucher_high_value`, `pos.redeem_voucher`, `pos.refund_no_receipt`, `pos.refund_voucher_to_cash`, `pos.rotate_qr_signing_key`, `pos.search_customer_cross_company`, `pos.search_customer_recent_purchases`, `pos.void_receipts`, `pos_orders.view`, `pos_orders.create`, `pos_orders.update`, `pos_orders.delete`, `purchase-quote-requests.delete`, `workshop.technicians.adjust_time_entries`, `workshop.technicians.approve_time_off`, **`reports.view`**.

**Seventeen of the 22 are currently granted to at least one non-`admin` template**, which is exactly why `deprecate()` emptying `templateDefaults` is a *behaviour* change and not a label: step 6's exact diff revokes each from every uncustomised template-linked role. The measured holders, so the reviewer is checking a table rather than a claim:

| Key | Held by (non-admin templates, post-0b) |
|---|---|
| `audit.view` | `accountant` |
| `catalog-cart.convert_so` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `catalog-cart.manage_all` | — |
| `inventory.receive` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `invoices.print` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier`, `operator` |
| `menus.view` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier`, `viewer` |
| `pos.issue_goodwill_voucher_high_value` | — |
| `pos.redeem_voucher` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier` |
| `pos.refund_no_receipt` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `pos.refund_voucher_to_cash` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `pos.rotate_qr_signing_key` | — |
| `pos.search_customer_cross_company` | — |
| `pos.search_customer_recent_purchases` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier` |
| `pos.void_receipts` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `pos_orders.view` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier` |
| `pos_orders.create` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier` |
| `pos_orders.update` | `manager`, `general_manager`, `cashier` |
| `pos_orders.delete` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `purchase-quote-requests.delete` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `workshop.technicians.adjust_time_entries` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `workshop.technicians.approve_time_off` | `manager`, `general_manager` |
| `reports.view` | — |

**Four keys are EXCLUDED and must never be deprecated:** `batches.delete` and `batches.recall` (wave 0b made both live route gates — the audit found them "dead" precisely *because* those routes had no gate, and `batches.recall` is additionally re-granted by the lane to `general_manager`), `products.import` (gates a live importer surface reachable from the FE), and **`coupons.view`** (its only consumers are frontend guards — `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx:3054`, `apps/web/src/hooks/usePermissions.ts:112` under a **fail-closed** `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` contract — and wave 0a gated the two coupon reads on it, so it is now enforced by the router too). **Deprecating a key waves 0a/0b just started using would be a self-inflicted 403.**

**Re-derivation is mandatory and it is a gate item.** The set is re-derived from the tree **after** waves 0a and 0b land and the result **must equal these 22**. A difference is a **finding to rule on**, not a silent update.

**`permissions:report-dead` scans FOUR roots, not one.** The audit's census scanned `apps/api` only, so a key whose only consumers are frontend guards read as dead — which is how `coupons.view` nearly shipped deprecated. Two honesty rules keep the scan meaningful:
1. a hit in the **generated map alone** is *not* evidence of use — it is generated from the catalogue, so every key is in it by construction; it is scanned only to prove the key exists;
2. a hit must be a **permission-shaped** use. The frontends contain i18n keys with identical spellings — `apps/pos/src/components/pos/TodaySalesPanel.tsx:308` is `t('reports.view')`, a translation key, and counting it would have kept a genuinely retired permission alive forever.

**Files:** `apps/api/app/Console/Commands/ReportDeadPermissions.php`, `apps/api/tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionReportDeadScopeTest.php`, plus the `deprecate()` wraps already printed in Task 1.4's manifest bodies.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the 22 `deprecate()` wraps are already in Task 1.4's bodies.** This step is the *verification*: re-derive the set from the tree and assert equality with the 22 above.

- [ ] **Step 2 — write `PermissionReportDeadScopeTest` first**, pinning (a) the four scan roots as a constant on the command, (b) that a `t('reports.view')` hit does **not** count, (c) that a hit in `permissionsMap.generated.ts` alone does **not** count, and (d) that a `can:` middleware hit, a `->can()` call and a `<RequirePermission permission="…">` attribute each **do** count. Fixtures are four tiny temp files under `storage/framework/testing/report-dead/` written by the test and passed via `--root=`.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the command, in full.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Symfony\Component\Finder\Finder;

/**
 * Report catalogue keys with no PERMISSION-SHAPED consumer in any of the four
 * roots. Reporting only: it never writes and never deprecates. The four roots
 * and the two honesty rules are pinned by PermissionReportDeadScopeTest,
 * because narrowing the scan back to apps/api is what nearly shipped
 * `coupons.view` deprecated (spec 4.9.3's ruling).
 */
final class ReportDeadPermissions extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:report-dead
        {--root=* : Override the scan roots (testing only; defaults to the four below)}
        {--json : Emit a JSON array instead of a table}';

    protected $description = 'List catalogue keys with no permission-shaped consumer in apps/api, apps/web/src, apps/pos/src or the generated map.';

    /** The FOUR roots, relative to the repository root. Narrowing this list is the defect this command exists to prevent. */
    public const SCAN_ROOTS = ['apps/api', 'apps/web/src', 'apps/pos/src'];

    /** Scanned only to prove a key EXISTS; a hit here is never evidence of use (honesty rule 1). */
    public const GENERATED_MAP = 'apps/web/src/hooks/permissionsMap.generated.ts';

    /**
     * A hit must be permission-shaped (honesty rule 2). `t('reports.view')` is a
     * translation key and matches none of these.
     *
     * @var list<non-empty-string>
     */
    private const CONSUMER_PATTERNS = [
        '/can:%s(?![A-Za-z0-9_.\-])/',                       // route middleware, incl. require.any.permission lists
        '/->\s*(can|cant|hasPermissionTo|hasAnyPermission|hasAllPermissions)\s*\(\s*[\'"]%s[\'"]/',
        '/Gate::\s*(authorize|allows|denies)\s*\(\s*[\'"]%s[\'"]/',
        '/(permission|permissions|anyOf|allOf)\s*[:=]\s*[\'"]%s[\'"]/',   // <RequirePermission permission="the-key">
        '/(hasPermission|can)\s*\(\s*[\'"]%s[\'"]/',                      // usePermissions()
        '/[\'"]%s[\'"]\s*,?\s*\]/',                                       // MODULE_PERMISSIONS arrays
    ];

    public function __construct(private readonly PermissionRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        /** @var list<string> $rootOption */
        $rootOption = $this->option('root');
        $roots = $rootOption !== [] ? $rootOption : array_map(static fn (string $r): string => base_path('../../'.$r), self::SCAN_ROOTS);

        $haystack = '';
        foreach ($roots as $root) {
            if (! is_dir($root)) {
                $this->warn("scan root missing, skipped: {$root}");

                continue;
            }
            $finder = (new Finder)->files()->in($root)
                ->name(['*.php', '*.ts', '*.tsx', '*.js', '*.jsx'])
                ->notPath('vendor')->notPath('node_modules')->notPath('dist')->notPath('build')
                ->notName('permissionsMap.generated.ts');   // honesty rule 1
            foreach ($finder as $file) {
                $haystack .= $file->getContents()."\n";
            }
        }

        $dead = [];
        foreach ($this->registry->keys() as $key) {
            $quoted = preg_quote($key, '/');
            $used = false;
            foreach (self::CONSUMER_PATTERNS as $pattern) {
                if (preg_match(sprintf($pattern, $quoted), $haystack) === 1) {
                    $used = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (! $used) {
                $dead[] = $key;
            }
        }
        sort($dead);

        if ((bool) $this->option('json')) {
            $this->line((string) json_encode($dead, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT | JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES | JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE));

            return self::SUCCESS;
        }

        $this->info(sprintf('%d catalogue keys have no permission-shaped consumer in %d roots:', count($dead), count($roots)));
        foreach ($dead as $key) {
            $definition = $this->registry->get($key);
            $this->line(sprintf('  %-52s module=%-18s deprecated=%s', $key, $definition?->module ?? '?', ($definition?->deprecated ?? false) ? 'yes' : 'NO'));
        }

        return self::SUCCESS;
    }
}
~~~

- [ ] **Step 4 — run it and compare to the 22.**

## Task 1.7: `SystemRoleTemplate` and the `roles` schema migration

**Timestamped after `2026_09_06_205000`**, and it must **not** write `tenant_id` — the W-LOT CHECK's `tenant_id IS NOT NULL` clause interacts with a NULL-team legacy role.

| Column | Type | Null | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| `is_system` | `boolean` | NO | `false` |
| `template_key` | `varchar(64)` | YES | `NULL` |
| `template_version` | `integer` | YES | `NULL` |
| `customised_at` | `timestamptz` | YES | `NULL` |

**Two partial uniques, because one index cannot express both topologies.**
- `roles_tenant_template_key_unique ON roles (tenant_id, template_key) WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL` — *"at most one role per template **per tenant**"*, the real invariant in both topologies and the only one that holds in compatibility mode, where every tenant's roles share one physical table and two tenant-scoped `manager` rows are legal today (the existing Spatie unique is `(tenant_id, name, guard_name)`).
- `roles_global_template_key_unique ON roles (template_key) WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL AND tenant_id IS NULL` — the NULL-team half. PostgreSQL treats every NULL as distinct, so the first index alone would permit two adopted NULL-team `manager` rows; this second one forbids exactly that without touching the tenant-scoped rows.

**Three CHECKs**: `template_key IS NULL OR is_system`; `customised_at IS NULL OR is_system`; `template_version IS NULL OR template_key IS NOT NULL`. The third exists because the pair without it permits `template_version = 7, template_key = NULL, is_system = false` — a state that contradicts the column's own meaning. Normal sync never creates it; a CHECK that permits a meaningless state is one that will eventually be handed one.

**The lane split, stated rather than assumed.** PostgreSQL is the production and the CI-PG topology; SQLite is the fast unit lane (`apps/api/phpunit.xml`). SQLite supports neither `ALTER TABLE … ADD CONSTRAINT` nor a partial unique index added after the fact in the shape PG uses, so the migration branches on `DB::getDriverName()`:

| Construct | PostgreSQL | SQLite equivalent shipped in the same migration |
|---|---|---|
| `roles_tenant_template_key_unique` | `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX … WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL` | **identical statement** — SQLite has supported partial indexes since 3.8.0, and the repository's SQLite is 3.4x. No branch needed. |
| `roles_global_template_key_unique` | same | **identical statement**. No branch needed. |
| the three CHECKs | `ALTER TABLE roles ADD CONSTRAINT … CHECK (…)` | **three `BEFORE INSERT` + three `BEFORE UPDATE` triggers** that `RAISE(ABORT, '<constraint name>')`, following the pattern the W-LOT migration already established for `roles_provisioning_source_check` — so the constraint *name* appears in the SQLite error message and one test asserts the same behaviour on both lanes. |
| `down()`'s refusal | one `SELECT count(*)` | identical |

**No value CHECK on `template_key`, and that is a decision, not an omission.** The eight-case list is expected to grow — a future vertical may ship a ninth template — and a value CHECK would make that growth a tenant migration on **every** database rather than an enum edit plus a sync, which is the whole point of Direction B. Validity is enforced where the value is written (`SystemRoleTemplate` is the only source of a `template_key` in production code; sync writes `$case->value` and never a string) plus a **schema-level ratchet test**, `RoleTemplateKeyDomainTest`, asserting over a live tenant database that `SELECT DISTINCT template_key FROM roles WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL` is a subset of `SystemRoleTemplate::cases()`.

**`down()` REFUSES while any row has `template_key IS NOT NULL`**, mirroring the lane's `down()` contract — so a rollback after a sync is a deliberate act that first requires clearing adoption, and never silently discards which roles were system roles.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — `SystemRoleTemplate`.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums;

/**
 * The eight shipped role templates. `template_key` on `roles` holds one of
 * these values and nothing else — there is deliberately NO value CHECK in the
 * schema (adding a ninth template must be an enum edit plus a sync, not a
 * migration on every tenant database), so this enum plus
 * RoleTemplateKeyDomainTest are the domain guard.
 *
 * GeneralManager REUSES lane/w-lot-a-1a's SystemRoleName::GeneralManager value.
 * SystemRoleTemplateAlignmentTest asserts the two are equal so they cannot
 * drift; wave 3 reconciles the two enums into one.
 */
enum SystemRoleTemplate: string
{
    case Admin = 'admin';
    case GeneralManager = 'general_manager';
    case Manager = 'manager';
    case Cashier = 'cashier';
    case Viewer = 'viewer';
    case Technician = 'technician';
    case Operator = 'operator';
    case Accountant = 'accountant';

    /** `admin` is the only template whose grant set is derived, not declared (registry->activeKeys()). */
    public function isDerived(): bool
    {
        return $this === self::Admin;
    }

    /** @return list<self> every template except `admin` — the ones step 6 applies a declared delta to. */
    public static function declared(): array
    {
        return array_values(array_filter(self::cases(), static fn (self $case): bool => ! $case->isDerived()));
    }
}
~~~

- [ ] **Step 2 — the migration, in full**, at `apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2026_09_18_100000_add_template_columns_to_roles.php`:

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

use Illuminate\Database\Migrations\Migration;
use Illuminate\Database\Schema\Blueprint;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;

/**
 * Wave 1, spec 5.2. Timestamped AFTER 2026_09_06_205000_add_provisioning_source_to_roles
 * so the W-LOT CHECK, partial unique and triggers are already in place and are
 * left byte-identical (RoleTemplateColumnsMigrationTest asserts that).
 *
 * This migration NEVER writes tenant_id.
 */
return new class extends Migration
{
    /** @var list<array{name: non-empty-string, check: non-empty-string}> */
    private const CHECKS = [
        ['name' => 'roles_template_key_requires_system', 'check' => 'template_key IS NULL OR is_system'],
        ['name' => 'roles_customised_at_requires_system', 'check' => 'customised_at IS NULL OR is_system'],
        ['name' => 'roles_template_version_requires_key', 'check' => 'template_version IS NULL OR template_key IS NOT NULL'],
    ];

    public function up(): void
    {
        Schema::table('roles', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->boolean('is_system')->default(false)->nullable(false);
            $table->string('template_key', 64)->nullable();
            $table->integer('template_version')->nullable();
            $table->timestampTz('customised_at')->nullable();
        });

        // Partial unique indexes. Both statements are valid on PostgreSQL AND on
        // SQLite >= 3.8.0, so there is no driver branch here.
        DB::statement('CREATE UNIQUE INDEX roles_tenant_template_key_unique ON roles (tenant_id, template_key) WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL');
        DB::statement('CREATE UNIQUE INDEX roles_global_template_key_unique ON roles (template_key) WHERE template_key IS NOT NULL AND tenant_id IS NULL');

        if (DB::getDriverName() === 'sqlite') {
            $this->createSqliteCheckTriggers();

            return;
        }

        foreach (self::CHECKS as $constraint) {
            DB::statement(sprintf(
                'ALTER TABLE roles ADD CONSTRAINT %s CHECK (%s)',
                $constraint['name'],
                $constraint['check'],
            ));
        }
    }

    public function down(): void
    {
        $adopted = (int) DB::table('roles')->whereNotNull('template_key')->count();
        if ($adopted > 0) {
            throw new RuntimeException(sprintf(
                'refusing to drop the roles template columns while %d role(s) carry a template_key. '
                .'Clear adoption deliberately first (UPDATE roles SET template_key = NULL, template_version = NULL, '
                .'customised_at = NULL, is_system = false), then re-run. Rolling back silently would discard which '
                .'roles were system roles.',
                $adopted,
            ));
        }

        if (DB::getDriverName() === 'sqlite') {
            foreach (self::CHECKS as $constraint) {
                DB::statement('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS '.$constraint['name'].'_insert');
                DB::statement('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS '.$constraint['name'].'_update');
            }
        } else {
            foreach (self::CHECKS as $constraint) {
                DB::statement('ALTER TABLE roles DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS '.$constraint['name']);
            }
        }

        DB::statement('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS roles_global_template_key_unique');
        DB::statement('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS roles_tenant_template_key_unique');

        Schema::table('roles', function (Blueprint $table): void {
            $table->dropColumn(['customised_at', 'template_version', 'template_key', 'is_system']);
        });
    }

    /**
     * SQLite has no ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT, so each CHECK becomes a
     * BEFORE INSERT + BEFORE UPDATE trigger that RAISE(ABORT, '<constraint name>').
     * The constraint NAME is the abort message, so one test asserts the same
     * observable behaviour on both lanes. Same pattern as the W-LOT migration's
     * roles_provisioning_source_check.
     */
    private function createSqliteCheckTriggers(): void
    {
        foreach (self::CHECKS as $constraint) {
            foreach (['insert', 'update'] as $event) {
                DB::statement(sprintf(
                    'CREATE TRIGGER %s_%s BEFORE %s ON roles FOR EACH ROW '
                    .'WHEN NOT (%s) BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, %s); END',
                    $constraint['name'],
                    $event,
                    strtoupper($event),
                    str_replace(
                        ['template_key', 'customised_at', 'template_version', 'is_system'],
                        ['NEW.template_key', 'NEW.customised_at', 'NEW.template_version', 'NEW.is_system'],
                        $constraint['check'],
                    ),
                    "'".$constraint['name']."'",
                ));
            }
        }
    }
};
~~~

- [ ] **Step 3 — `RoleTemplateColumnsMigrationTest`** (PG **and** SQLite, one data provider over the driver): the two indexes exist with the exact predicates (PG: `pg_indexes.indexdef`; SQLite: `sqlite_master.sql`); each CHECK refuses its meaningless state **with the constraint name in the message on both drivers**; `down()` refuses while a `template_key` is set and succeeds after it is cleared; and `roles_provisioning_source_check`, `roles_provisioning_source_team_unique` and both W-LOT SQLite triggers are **byte-identical before and after** the migration (compare `pg_get_constraintdef` / `sqlite_master.sql` strings).

## Task 1.8: `RoleMarkerRepository`, `TemplateDelta` and the exact-diff `TemplateDeltaApplier`

**The marker predicate is `private` on the lane's seeder** (`RolesAndPermissionsSeeder::markedTenantCarriesWlota1aDelta()`) and therefore uncallable. It becomes a small **public** collaborator that reproduces the lane's predicate exactly — the `Schema::hasColumn('roles', 'provisioning_source')` guard, the tenant-team filter, `guard_name = 'sanctum'`, `whereRaw('provisioning_source = ?')` — **minus the hard-coded role name**. **The lane's private method is left exactly as it is.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Repositories;

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Schema;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

/**
 * The lane's private marker predicate, re-expressed for external callers.
 *
 * PermissionSyncService uses it for EXACTLY ONE decision: whether a
 * general_manager it CREATES must also carry provisioning_source. It never
 * clears the marker, never changes it, never widens it to another role or
 * another source (spec 4.3.0a rule 3).
 */
final readonly class RoleMarkerRepository
{
    public function __construct(private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar) {}

    /** True when this tenant already carries a role marked with $source. */
    public function tenantCarriesMarker(string $source): bool
    {
        if (! Schema::hasColumn('roles', 'provisioning_source')) {
            return false;
        }

        return Role::query()
            ->where(config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key'), $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId())
            ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
            ->whereRaw('provisioning_source = ?', [$source])
            ->exists();
    }
}
~~~

**`TemplateDeltaApplier` is the ONE implementation of "apply a template to a role", with two callers** — sync's step 6 and `RoleController::reapplyTemplate`. That is what rev 2 of the spec's deleted `syncRole()` was reaching for and what its signature could not deliver: `sync(string, bool)` and `syncRole(string, string, bool)` would have been two orderings of the same rules.

**It is an EXACT diff, not an add-plus-deprecation-sweep.** The role's grant set is made **equal** to the template's: every key the template declares and the role lacks is granted, **and every key the role holds that the template does not declare is revoked — deprecated or active.** That is safe **here and only here**: an *uncustomised* template-linked role is one whose grant set has never diverged from its template, so by construction such a role *is* its template plus whatever sync itself put there, and every extra key is a key the platform added and has since retracted — never a tenant's choice.

**The `template_version` write is CONDITIONAL, and it is counted** (plan gate r1, BLOCKER B1-3). Rev 1 issued `DB::table('roles')->update(['template_version' => $registryVersion])` **unconditionally**, which writes one row per non-`admin` template on **every** run — seven writes on a second run, directly contradicting the invariant that `ALREADY_CURRENT` means every `mutations` counter zero **and** zero database writes. The applier now writes the version **only when it differs**, reports `versionChanged` on the DTO, and `PermissionSyncResult` counts it inside `templates_applied` so the marker's numbers and its outcome token cannot disagree.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs;

/** The result of applying one template to one role. */
final readonly class TemplateDelta
{
    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $added
     * @param  list<string>  $removed
     */
    public function __construct(
        public array $added,
        public array $removed,
        public ?int $fromVersion,
        public int $toVersion,
        public bool $versionChanged,
    ) {}

    /** True when this apply wrote ANY row — the clause the zero-write invariant needs. */
    public function wrote(): bool
    {
        return $this->added !== [] || $this->removed !== [] || $this->versionChanged;
    }
}
~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\TemplateDelta;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use LogicException;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;

/**
 * "Apply a template to a role", once, for two callers: PermissionSyncService's
 * step 6 and RoleController::reapplyTemplate.
 *
 * CALLER OBLIGATION: this method assumes it is already inside a transaction
 * whose first statement was PermissionWriteLock::acquire(), and that $role was
 * read under FOR UPDATE ordered roles.name ASC, roles.id ASC. It does NOT take
 * the lock itself — a helper that took its own lock would let a caller believe
 * it was serialized when its OTHER reads were not. That is why this method is
 * declared in PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest's LOCK_INHERITED_FROM map (its
 * two entry points are named there), not in LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS: wave 0b's
 * PermissionWriterCensus proves the caller relationship from the AST rather
 * than trusting this docblock.
 */
final readonly class TemplateDeltaApplier
{
    public function __construct(private PermissionRegistry $registry) {}

    public function applyTo(Role $role, int $registryVersion): TemplateDelta
    {
        $templateKey = $role->getAttribute('template_key');
        if (! is_string($templateKey) || $templateKey === '') {
            throw new LogicException('TemplateDeltaApplier requires a template-linked role.');
        }

        /** @var list<string> $target */
        $target = $this->registry->templateGrants()[$templateKey] ?? [];
        /** @var list<string> $current */
        $current = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();

        $added = array_values(array_diff($target, $current));
        $removed = array_values(array_diff($current, $target));

        $from = $role->getAttribute('template_version');
        $fromVersion = is_int($from) ? $from : null;
        $versionChanged = $fromVersion !== $registryVersion;

        if ($added !== [] || $removed !== []) {
            // Set EQUALITY, in one call: syncPermissions replaces the pivot set.
            $role->syncPermissions($target);
        }

        // CONDITIONAL. An unconditional UPDATE writes one row per template on
        // EVERY run and breaks the zero-write clause of ALREADY_CURRENT
        // (plan gate r1, BLOCKER B1-3).
        if ($versionChanged) {
            DB::table('roles')->where('id', $role->getKey())->update(['template_version' => $registryVersion]);
        }

        return new TemplateDelta(
            added: $added,
            removed: $removed,
            fromVersion: $fromVersion,
            toVersion: $registryVersion,
            versionChanged: $versionChanged,
        );
    }
}
~~~

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — write the three tests first**, then the three classes.
- [ ] **Step 2 — `TemplateDeltaApplierExactnessTest`** (PG):
      - `an_uncustomised_role_equals_its_template_after_apply` — seed the role with **one extra active key** and **one missing key**, assert set equality **in both directions** afterwards, and assert `added` / `removed` name exactly those two;
      - `a_second_apply_at_the_same_version_writes_nothing` — **asserted by query-log count over the whole call**, which is the B1-3 red: run the applier twice at the same `$registryVersion` and assert the second call issues **zero** `insert`/`update`/`delete` statements. Rev 1's applier fails this on the `roles.template_version` UPDATE; paste that failure into the handback as the named red.
      - `a_customised_role_is_byte_identical_after_apply` — a customised role is never a delta target and the applier must never be called for one; asserted by query-log count from the *caller's* side in Task 1.10's tests.
- [ ] **Step 3 — `TemplateDeltaApplierParityTest`**: a *Re-apply template* and a sync produce **byte-identical** grant sets for the same role and the same registry version (written now against the applier directly; Task 1.12 extends it through the controller).
- [ ] **Step 4 — `RoleMarkerRepositoryTest`**: true on a marked tenant, false on an unmarked one, false when the column is absent (SQLite lane, before the W-LOT migration), and **no role name is hard-coded** — a marked `manager` would also return true, which is the difference from the lane's private predicate.

## Task 1.9: `RoleSyncedV1`, the `AuditEvent` guard, `recordTenantWide()` and the subscriber

**This task lands BEFORE sync**, because sync's steps 6 and 7 emit `RoleSyncedV1` and a commit that emits an event class that does not exist is red.

**Sync is not an operator, so it may not emit `RoleUpdated`.** That class requires a non-null `companyId` **and** a non-null `actorUserId`, and sync is tenant-wide, runs from a console/deploy context, and has neither. The existing consumer enforces both (`DomainEventSubscriber::persistEvent()` takes `string $companyId` at `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Listeners/DomainEventSubscriber.php:1186-1193` and hands it to `AuditService::record(companyId: string …)` at `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Services/AuditService.php:43-51`). Reusing the class would mean **inventing a company and an actor for a write that has neither**, which is worse than a new event: an audit row naming a company sync did not act in is a false statement in a compliance chain.

**The persisted NAME carries the version too, not only the class:** `identity.role.synced.v1` end to end, and the successor is `RoleSyncedV2` / `identity.role.synced.v2`. The tree already resolves this the same way (`RewardRedeemedV2` pairs with `.v2`).

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Events;

use App\Shared\Domain\Events\DomainEvent;

/**
 * Emitted by permissions:sync and by Re-apply template. IMMUTABLE FOREVER
 * (CLAUDE.md rule 8): a later change is RoleSyncedV2 / identity.role.synced.v2,
 * never an edit to this class.
 */
final class RoleSyncedV1 extends DomainEvent
{
    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $added
     * @param  list<string>  $removed
     */
    public function __construct(
        public readonly int $roleId,
        public readonly string $roleName,
        public readonly ?string $templateKey,   // null for a custom role receiving a replacement grant
        public readonly array $added,
        public readonly array $removed,
        public readonly string $cause,          // 'template_delta' | 'deprecation_replacement' | 'template_reapply'
        public readonly ?int $fromTemplateVersion,
        public readonly ?int $toTemplateVersion,
        public readonly string $tenantId,       // the actor is the deploy, so the TENANT is the scope
        public readonly ?string $actorUserId,   // non-null ONLY for the operator-initiated re-apply
        public readonly string $occurredAtIso,
    ) {
        parent::__construct((string) $roleId);
    }

    public function getEventName(): string
    {
        return 'identity.role.synced.v1';
    }

    /** @return array<string, mixed> */
    public function getAuditPayload(): array
    {
        return [
            'role_id' => $this->roleId,
            'role_name' => $this->roleName,
            'template_key' => $this->templateKey,
            'added' => $this->added,
            'removed' => $this->removed,
            'cause' => $this->cause,
            'from_template_version' => $this->fromTemplateVersion,
            'to_template_version' => $this->toTemplateVersion,
            'actor_user_id' => $this->actorUserId,
            'occurred_at' => $this->occurredAtIso,
        ];
    }
}
~~~

**Where it is written, and the TWO-line change that lets it be.** A tenant-wide event has **no company**, and `audit_events.company_id` is already nullable (`apps/api/database/migrations/tenant/2025_11_30_140001_add_company_id_to_audit_events.php:19`) — but it cannot simply be passed as `null`, because `AuditEvent::__construct` populates **nothing at all** unless `$companyId !== null` (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Compliance/Domain/AuditEvent.php:103-136`): a null company id today produces an **empty, unhashed row, silently**. So, precisely:

1. the guard at `dev:…:103` becomes `if ($companyId !== null || isset($attributes['tenant_id']))`;
2. **and `dev:…:104` becomes `$this->companyId = $companyId ?? '';`.** Rev 1 said *"nothing else in that branch changes"*. That is **wrong and would not compile at runtime**: the property is declared `public string $companyId = '';` at `dev:…:71`, so assigning `null` to it throws a `TypeError` on the first tenant-wide event. `''` is the correct sentinel — it is the property's own declared default, `calculateHash()` (`dev:…:236-240`) reads `$this->companyId` and therefore hashes a stable empty string, and the persisted `attributes['company_id']` at `dev:…:127` stays the **real** `null`, which is what the nullable column and every `whereNull('company_id')` reader expect.
3. Everything else in that branch is genuinely unchanged — the `tenant_id` optimisation path at `dev:…:114-115` already accepts an explicit tenant id and already bypasses the `Company::find()` throw at `dev:…:117-120`.
4. `AuditService` gains **`recordTenantWide()`**, mirroring `record()`'s parameter shape with the actor **as an explicit, named parameter in the same position `record()` puts it** (plan gate r1, BLOCKER B1-6 — rev 1's signature had no `$actorUserId` and its body passed `userId: $actorUserId`, which does not compile):

~~~php
    /**
     * Record a TENANT-WIDE audit event — one with no company and, on a deploy
     * run, no actor. Mirrors record() exactly, including the position of the
     * nullable actor, and does NOT widen record()'s own `string $companyId`,
     * so no existing caller changes behaviour.
     *
     * @param  array<string, mixed>  $payload
     * @param  array<string, mixed>  $metadata
     */
    public function recordTenantWide(
        string $tenantId,
        ?string $actorUserId,
        string $eventType,
        string $aggregateType,
        string $aggregateId,
        array $payload = [],
        array $metadata = []
    ): AuditEvent {
        $event = new AuditEvent(
            companyId: null,
            userId: $actorUserId,
            eventType: $eventType,
            aggregateType: $aggregateType,
            aggregateId: $aggregateId,
            payload: $payload,
            metadata: $metadata,
            attributes: array_filter([
                'tenant_id' => $tenantId,
                'impersonator_id' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->operator_id,
                'impersonation_session_id' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->session_id,
                'impersonation_event_id' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->audit_event_id,
                'impersonation_sequence' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->audit_sequence,
                'impersonation_previous_hash' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->audit_previous_hash,
                'impersonation_hash' => $this->impersonationContext->current()?->audit_hash,
            ], static fn (mixed $value): bool => $value !== null)
        );

        $event->save();

        return $event;
    }
~~~

5. `DomainEventSubscriber` gains a `persistTenantWideEvent()` sibling to `persistEvent()` and **registers `RoleSyncedV1` in `subscribe()` in the same commit that introduces the class** — an event class the subscriber does not register writes **no audit row, silently**, which is why the test asserts a **row lands** rather than that the event was dispatched:

~~~php
    /**
     * Persist a TENANT-WIDE event (no company) to the audit log. Sibling of
     * persistEvent(); the actor comes from the EVENT, not from Auth::id(),
     * because a deploy-run sync has no authenticated user and a re-apply does.
     *
     * @param  array<string, mixed>  $payload
     */
    private function persistTenantWideEvent(
        DomainEvent $event,
        string $tenantId,
        ?string $actorUserId,
        string $aggregateType,
        string $aggregateId,
        string $eventType,
        array $payload,
    ): void {
        try {
            $this->auditService->recordTenantWide(
                tenantId: $tenantId,
                actorUserId: $actorUserId,
                eventType: $eventType,
                aggregateType: $aggregateType,
                aggregateId: $aggregateId,
                payload: $payload,
                metadata: [
                    'event_class' => $event::class,
                    'occurred_at' => $event->occurredAt()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s.u'),
                ],
            );
        } catch (Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('Failed to persist tenant-wide audit event', [
                'event_type' => $eventType,
                'tenant_id' => $tenantId,
                'error' => $e->getMessage(),
            ]);
        }
    }

    public function handleRoleSynced(RoleSyncedV1 $event): void
    {
        $this->persistTenantWideEvent(
            event: $event,
            tenantId: $event->tenantId,
            actorUserId: $event->actorUserId,
            aggregateType: 'Role',
            aggregateId: (string) $event->roleId,
            eventType: $event->getEventName(),
            payload: $event->getAuditPayload(),
        );
    }
~~~

and, in `subscribe()`'s *Identity events* block (`dev:…:1259-1262`), one line beside `RoleAssigned` / `RoleRemoved`:
