## Task 1.10: `PermissionSyncService`, both commands, the dry-run sentinel — and the deletion of all eight wave-0b stopgap artefacts. **ONE commit.**

> **This is one commit by ruling** (plan gate r1, BLOCKER B1-4). Wave 0b's binding cutover promise is that the ensure files and their deploy rows disappear **in the commit that introduces their replacement**; rev 1 split them across Tasks 9, 10 and 14, and additionally had Task 10 reuse 0b's `DryRunRollback` while Task 14 deleted it with no replacement. The commit below lands the replacement and removes the stopgap atomically, and it ships wave 1's own sentinel in the same breath, so there is never a moment at which the dry-run path has no rollback carrier.

### One public method

`sync(string $tenantId, bool $write): PermissionSyncResult`. There is no `syncRole()`: with the template-migration machinery retired there is no step 5 to run for one template, and the remaining single-role work is exactly the step-6 delta for one already-adopted role — which does not need a second public entry point, a second transaction shape, or a second set of counters.

### The nine steps, in this order

1. **Rename.** For each `(from, to)` where a `permissions` row named `from` exists and none named `to` exists: `UPDATE permissions SET name = :to WHERE name = :from AND guard_name = 'sanctum'`. **In place** — the primary key never changes, so every `role_has_permissions` and `model_has_permissions` row survives untouched and **no grant is lost**. If **both** exist the run is `BLOCKED` with `reason=rename_target_exists` (a human must decide which grants win). If **neither** exists the entry is a no-op and is **reported as stale**, so the map can be trimmed. **A renamed key can never re-enter:** step 2 creates only `registry->keys()`, and `no_manifest_declares_a_rename_source_key` proves no manifest declares a `from`.
2. **Create.** For every `registry->keys()` **including deprecated** — a deprecated permission keeps its row for one release so a custom role referencing it does not break. The implementation is `exists()` then `Permission::create()`, **not** `firstOrCreate`, for 0b's stated reason: `firstOrCreate` cannot report which branch it took (so `created=` is uncountable) and it **writes on the dry-run path**.
3. **Orphans.** A `permissions` row present in the database and absent from the registry is **reported, never deleted**. Deleting it would cascade its grants away, and a row can legitimately be a leftover from a tenant that has not yet received a deploy that removed a module. **This step writes nothing**, which is why it may sit before adoption. Its count is a **gauge**. `permissions:prune-orphans --confirm` is wave 3 and is a deliberate operator action.
4. **Adopt** — **the first step that touches `roles`, and it writes no grant.** For each **existing** role whose `name` matches a `SystemRoleTemplate` case (resolved with the NULL-team-tolerant predicate) **and whose `template_key IS NULL`**: compute its current grant set, compare through `LegacyRoleBaseline::matchesVersion0()`, and adopt with `is_system = true`, `template_key = <name>`, `template_version = 0`, and `customised_at = NULL` (equal) or `customised_at = now()` (different). Counted as `adopted_pristine` / `adopted_customised`, logged per role, listed in `--dry-run`. **Adoption is one-way and once**: a role that already carries a `template_key` is never re-classified. Roles whose name matches no template stay `is_system = false` and are never touched.

   > **`customised_at = now()` for the "different" branch is deliberately the CONSERVATIVE direction.** It can only ever *withhold* a delta from a tenant that had in fact not customised, which surfaces as a visible banner and a one-click *Re-apply template*. The opposite error — adopting a genuinely customised role as pristine and then silently re-granting what the tenant removed — is precisely I-20 in a new costume.

5. **Create missing template roles.** For each `SystemRoleTemplate` case with no resolvable role in this tenant, create it with `tenant_id = :tenantId`, `guard_name = 'sanctum'`, `is_system = true`, `template_key`, `template_version = <registry version>`, `customised_at = NULL`, and **no grants yet** — step 6 grants. Counted as `roles_created`. **`general_manager` is created here like any other template role, with one addition:** when `roleMarkers->tenantCarriesMarker('w-lot-a-1a')` is `false`, the insert **also** sets `provisioning_source = 'w-lot-a-1a'` and `marker_written` becomes 1. Every clause of the lane's CHECK holds by construction on this path — sync always sets a tenant id and always this name and guard — so the write cannot violate it, and the partial unique is satisfied because sync reaches this branch only when the tenant has no marked role.
6. **Template deltas — an EXACT diff on uncustomised template-linked roles.** For each **non-`admin`** template, through `TemplateDeltaApplier`: **lock and re-read first** (`FOR UPDATE`, `roles.name` ASC then `roles.id` ASC, then read `customised_at` and the grant set **from that locked read**); **skip** the role entirely if the re-read `customised_at IS NOT NULL` (counted in the `templates_skipped_customised` **gauge**, `template_version` left where it was, which is what lets the Roles UI say *"this role is 3 versions behind"*); otherwise make the grant set **equal** the template's; set `template_version` **only when it differs**; emit one **`RoleSyncedV1`** per role whose grant set actually changed.
7. **Deprecation replacement grants** — **the one case in which sync writes a custom role.** For every deprecated definition naming a `replacedBy`, grant the successor to every role currently holding the deprecated key, **custom roles included**, and emit a `RoleSyncedV1` per affected role. A deprecation with `replacedBy: null` writes nothing anywhere — **and all 22 of wave 1's deprecations carry `replacedBy: null`, so this step is a provable no-op in this wave and exists for the next one.** The reason it is not a carve-out: a deprecated key stops being granted by any template and will eventually be pruned, so a custom role not given the successor **loses the capability on prune day, silently**, which is exactly what D2 forbids.
8. **Admin.** The role whose `template_key = 'admin'` is set to exactly `registry->activeKeys()` with `syncPermissions()`. This is the one place a full replace is correct, and it is what replaces `Permission::all()` — after it, the catalogue is once again the boundary of what `admin` holds, and a stray row inserted out of band is no longer silently attached. Counted as `admin_changes`, **including removals**. **A tenant with no resolvable `admin` after step 5 is `FAILED` with `reason=admin_role_missing`** — the wave-1 home of 0b's `AdminRoleMissing` sentinel, which is deleted with the rest of the stopgap.
9. **Cache — AFTER commit.** `DB::afterCommit(fn () => $this->permissionRegistrar->forgetCachedPermissions())`, registered **inside** the transaction so it fires once the commit has landed, and it runs **while the permissions team id is still set to this tenant**, because the S-1 key scheme derives the cache namespace from it. A `finally` inside the transaction callback executes **before** Laravel commits, leaving a window in which another request rebuilds and re-caches the *old* snapshot which then outlives the commit — which is the defect this step's ordering exists to prevent.

### The result marker

```
PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=<uuid> mode=APPLY|DRY_RUN outcome=APPLIED|ALREADY_CURRENT|BLOCKED|FAILED
                 mutations: renamed=<n> created=<n> adopted_pristine=<n> adopted_customised=<n>
                            roles_created=<n> templates_applied=<n> templates_removed=<n>
                            replacements_granted=<n> admin_changes=<n> marker_written=<n>
                 gauges:    orphans=<n> templates_skipped_customised=<n>
                 reason=<token>
```

**Two kinds of number, and only one of them can be zero on a re-run.** The ten **`mutations`** counters are rows this run **wrote** — all zero, always, on a re-run. The two **`gauges`** are state this run **observed** — **unconstrained**; on a tenant with one customised `manager` and one orphan row, `orphans=1 templates_skipped_customised=1` is reported on **every** run forever, and that is not a failure. `outcome=ALREADY_CURRENT` is defined as *every `mutations` counter zero **AND** zero database writes*, so the marker's outcome token and its numbers cannot disagree. `markerLine(string $tenantId, bool $write)` renders `mode=` itself, so the per-tenant command and the fleet command emit **one** shape — 0b rev 2's minor fix, adopted.

**Why the second run is `ALREADY_CURRENT` — re-derived, not asserted.** **1** every `from` is gone and nothing re-created it → `renamed=0`; **2** every key exists → `created=0`; **3** the orphan set is a **read** and its count is a gauge; **4** every template-named role carries a `template_key` → `adopted_*=0`; **5** every case resolves → `roles_created=0`, `marker_written=0`; **6** every uncustomised template role already **equals** its template **and already carries the current `template_version`, so the conditional write does not fire** → `templates_applied=0`, `templates_removed=0`, while `templates_skipped_customised` reports the same non-zero number as the first run whenever the tenant has a customised role; **7** every role holding a deprecated key already holds its successor (vacuously, in this wave) → `replacements_granted=0`; **8** `syncPermissions(activeKeys())` on a role that already equals that set writes no pivot row → `admin_changes=0`; **9** the flush is unconditional and is not a counter.

**Exit codes:** `0` applied or already-current, `1` failed (**nothing written** — the whole run is one transaction), `2` blocked (a rename collision, an ambiguous legacy role, or compatibility mode with `--tenant`).

**Compatibility mode.** With `config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant') === false`, `permissions:sync` **refuses `--tenant`** (exit 2, `reason=compatibility_mode_is_global`) and runs **steps 1–4 only**, once globally, reporting `templates_applied=0 templates_removed=0 roles_created=0 marker_written=0`. **Adoption runs there too** — it is a DB-level classification of `roles` rows, not a template application, and skipping it would leave every system role at `is_system = false`, which is exactly the state wave 2's rename/delete protection and both floors depend on. **Step 5 in particular must NOT run there**: creating one tenant's `general_manager` in a shared `roles` table provisions it for everybody, and writing the marker there would additionally claim, falsely, that every tenant in the schema carries the lane's end state. This is **stated rather than silently degraded**, because a silent degrade is exactly how I-20 happened.

### Step 1 — the outcome enum, the sentinel and the result DTO

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums;

enum PermissionSyncOutcome: string
{
    case Applied = 'APPLIED';
    case AlreadyCurrent = 'ALREADY_CURRENT';
    case Blocked = 'BLOCKED';
    case Failed = 'FAILED';

    public function isGreen(): bool
    {
        return $this === self::Applied || $this === self::AlreadyCurrent;
    }

    /** 0 green, 1 failed, 2 blocked — identical on the applying and the dry-run path. */
    public function exitCode(): int
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::Applied, self::AlreadyCurrent => 0,
            self::Failed => 1,
            self::Blocked => 2,
        };
    }
}
~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\PermissionSyncResult;
use RuntimeException;

/**
 * WAVE 1's dry-run sentinel — the replacement for wave 0b's DryRunRollback,
 * shipped in the SAME commit that deletes it (plan gate r1, BLOCKER B1-4).
 *
 * A dry run is WRITE-free, not FAILURE-free. Throwing this from inside the
 * transaction rolls every speculative write back while carrying the computed
 * classification out, so the reported outcome, reason and counters are exactly
 * what an applying run would have produced — including BLOCKED (exit 2) and
 * FAILED (exit 1).
 *
 * It is ALSO the rollback carrier on the APPLYING path when step 1 detects a
 * rename collision: a BLOCKED run must write nothing, and rolling back through
 * the same sentinel keeps one exit from the transaction instead of two. The
 * name is kept because the dry run is its primary use; if a later wave adds a
 * second blocked cause, rename it to SyncRollback and update both call sites.
 */
final class SyncDryRunRollback extends RuntimeException
{
    public function __construct(public readonly PermissionSyncResult $result)
    {
        parent::__construct('dry run: rolling back');
    }
}
~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs;

use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\PermissionSyncOutcome;

/**
 * The accumulating result of one sync. MUTABLE by design: the nine private step
 * methods each take and return it, so a reviewer can read step 4 without
 * reading step 6.
 *
 * TEN mutations (rows this run WROTE — all zero on a re-run, always) and TWO
 * gauges (state this run OBSERVED — unconstrained on a re-run). Nothing may
 * assert a gauge is zero.
 */
final class PermissionSyncResult
{
    public int $renamed = 0;
    public int $created = 0;
    public int $adoptedPristine = 0;
    public int $adoptedCustomised = 0;
    public int $rolesCreated = 0;
    public int $templatesApplied = 0;
    public int $templatesRemoved = 0;
    public int $replacementsGranted = 0;
    public int $adminChanges = 0;
    public int $markerWritten = 0;

    public int $orphans = 0;
    public int $templatesSkippedCustomised = 0;

    public PermissionSyncOutcome $outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::AlreadyCurrent;
    public string $reason = 'already_current';

    /** @var list<string> per-role adoption/skip/delta log, rendered by --dry-run and by the handback */
    public array $log = [];

    /** @var list<string> rename entries whose `from` and `to` are BOTH absent — the map can be trimmed */
    public array $staleRenames = [];

    /** The conjunction the ALREADY_CURRENT definition needs. Gauges are deliberately absent. */
    public function hasMutations(): bool
    {
        return $this->renamed + $this->created + $this->adoptedPristine + $this->adoptedCustomised
            + $this->rolesCreated + $this->templatesApplied + $this->templatesRemoved
            + $this->replacementsGranted + $this->adminChanges + $this->markerWritten > 0;
    }

    public function blocked(string $reason): self
    {
        $this->outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::Blocked;
        $this->reason = $reason;

        return $this;
    }

    public function failed(string $reason): self
    {
        $this->outcome = PermissionSyncOutcome::Failed;
        $this->reason = $reason;

        return $this;
    }

    /** Settle the outcome from the counters. Never overrides a BLOCKED or FAILED verdict. */
    public function settle(): self
    {
        if ($this->outcome === PermissionSyncOutcome::Blocked || $this->outcome === PermissionSyncOutcome::Failed) {
            return $this;
        }
        $this->outcome = $this->hasMutations() ? PermissionSyncOutcome::Applied : PermissionSyncOutcome::AlreadyCurrent;
        $this->reason = $this->hasMutations() ? 'applied' : 'already_current';

        return $this;
    }

    /** ONE shape for both commands — 0b rev 2's markerLine(tenantId, write) fix, adopted. */
    public function markerLine(string $tenantId, bool $write): string
    {
        return sprintf(
            'PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=%s mode=%s outcome=%s '
            .'mutations: renamed=%d created=%d adopted_pristine=%d adopted_customised=%d '
            .'roles_created=%d templates_applied=%d templates_removed=%d '
            .'replacements_granted=%d admin_changes=%d marker_written=%d '
            .'gauges: orphans=%d templates_skipped_customised=%d reason=%s',
            $tenantId,
            $write ? 'APPLY' : 'DRY_RUN',
            $this->outcome->value,
            $this->renamed, $this->created, $this->adoptedPristine, $this->adoptedCustomised,
            $this->rolesCreated, $this->templatesApplied, $this->templatesRemoved,
            $this->replacementsGranted, $this->adminChanges, $this->markerWritten,
            $this->orphans, $this->templatesSkippedCustomised,
            $this->reason,
        );
    }
}
~~~

### Step 2 — the service, nine private steps

> **`Clock` is the spec's name, and the repository has no such abstraction today** (`grep -i clock apps/api/app` returns only `ClockAnomalyDetector`). Use the repository's own time source rather than inventing an interface for one caller: inject `Illuminate\Support\DateFactory` (Laravel's `Date` facade backing, which `Carbon::setTestNow()` already controls in tests) and read `->now()`. If a `Clock` contract lands later, this is a one-line swap. **Record the substitution in the handback** — it is a deliberate deviation from the spec's signature, and a gate will ask.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\PermissionSyncResult;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\LegacyRoleBaseline;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRenameMap;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\TemplateDeltaApplier;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\SystemRoleTemplate;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Events\RoleSyncedV1;
use App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Locks\PermissionWriteLock;
use App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Repositories\RoleMarkerRepository;
use App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry;
use DomainException;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Events\Dispatcher;
use Illuminate\Database\Query\Builder as QueryBuilder;
use Illuminate\Support\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\DateFactory;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Throwable;

/**
 * The catalogue sync. ONE public method, nine private steps, one transaction,
 * one lock — wave 0b's PermissionWriteLock, unchanged.
 *
 * ADOPTION (step 4) RUNS BEFORE ANY GRANT WRITE. Running a role-mutating step
 * first is what let an earlier revision re-add a grant a tenant had removed,
 * making a customised role compare equal and be adopted pristine.
 *
 * This class references NO symbol from lane/w-lot-a-1a — not
 * LotActionPermissionDelta, not LotActionPermissionActivation, not any
 * TemplateMigration* class. PermissionSyncServiceTest::sync_references_no_lot_action_symbol
 * asserts it statically.
 */
final readonly class PermissionSyncService
{
    private const GUARD = 'sanctum';

    private const MARKER_SOURCE = 'w-lot-a-1a';

    public function __construct(
        private PermissionRegistry $registry,
        private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
        private PermissionRenameMap $renameMap,
        private LegacyRoleBaseline $legacyBaseline,
        private RoleMarkerRepository $roleMarkers,
        private PermissionWriteLock $writeLock,
        private TemplateDeltaApplier $applier,
        private Dispatcher $events,
        private DateFactory $dates,
    ) {}

    public function sync(string $tenantId, bool $write): PermissionSyncResult
    {
        $previousTeamId = $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($tenantId);

        try {
            return DB::transaction(function () use ($tenantId, $write): PermissionSyncResult {
                // The lock is the FIRST statement in the transaction.
                $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

                $result = new PermissionSyncResult;
                $perTenant = (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant');

                $this->stepRename($result, $write);
                if ($result->outcome->value === 'BLOCKED') {
                    throw new SyncDryRunRollback($result);   // rolls back; the caller re-reads the carried result
                }
                $this->stepCreate($result, $write);
                $this->stepOrphans($result);
                $roles = $this->resolveTemplateRoles($tenantId);
                $this->stepAdopt($result, $roles, $write);

                if ($perTenant) {
                    $roles = $this->stepCreateMissingTemplateRoles($result, $roles, $tenantId, $write);
                    $this->stepTemplateDeltas($result, $tenantId, $write);
                    $this->stepDeprecationReplacements($result, $tenantId, $write);
                    $this->stepAdmin($result, $tenantId, $write);
                } else {
                    $result->log[] = 'compatibility_mode: steps 5-8 skipped (shared roles table)';
                }

                $this->stepCacheAfterCommit();

                $result->settle();

                if (! $write) {
                    // WRITE-free, not FAILURE-free: the sentinel carries the
                    // classification out of the rolled-back transaction.
                    throw new SyncDryRunRollback($result);
                }

                return $result;
            });
        } catch (SyncDryRunRollback $rollback) {
            return $rollback->result;
        } catch (DomainException $blocked) {
            return (new PermissionSyncResult)->blocked($blocked->getMessage());
        } catch (Throwable $failure) {
            return (new PermissionSyncResult)->failed('exception:'.$failure->getMessage());
        } finally {
            $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($previousTeamId);
        }
    }

    // ---- step 1 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepRename(PermissionSyncResult $result, bool $write): void
    {
        foreach ($this->renameMap->entries() as $entry) {
            $fromExists = $this->permissionExists($entry['from']);
            $toExists = $this->permissionExists($entry['to']);

            if ($fromExists && $toExists) {
                $result->blocked('rename_target_exists:'.$entry['from'].'->'.$entry['to']);

                return;
            }
            if (! $fromExists) {
                if (! $toExists) {
                    $result->staleRenames[] = $entry['from'].'->'.$entry['to'];
                }

                continue;
            }

            $result->renamed++;
            $result->log[] = 'renamed '.$entry['from'].' -> '.$entry['to'];
            if ($write) {
                // IN PLACE: the primary key never changes, so no grant pivot is rewritten.
                DB::table('permissions')
                    ->where('name', $entry['from'])
                    ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
                    ->update(['name' => $entry['to']]);
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- step 2 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepCreate(PermissionSyncResult $result, bool $write): void
    {
        foreach ($this->registry->keys() as $key) {   // deprecated INCLUDED
            if ($this->permissionExists($key)) {
                continue;
            }
            $result->created++;
            if ($write) {
                // exists() + create(), never firstOrCreate: firstOrCreate cannot
                // report which branch it took and it WRITES on the dry-run path.
                Permission::create(['name' => $key, 'guard_name' => self::GUARD]);
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- step 3 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepOrphans(PermissionSyncResult $result): void
    {
        $known = $this->registry->keys();
        /** @var list<string> $present */
        $present = DB::table('permissions')->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)->pluck('name')->all();
        $orphans = array_values(array_diff($present, $known));
        sort($orphans);

        $result->orphans = count($orphans);   // a GAUGE: reported, never deleted, never asserted zero
        foreach ($orphans as $orphan) {
            $result->log[] = 'orphan '.$orphan;
        }
    }

    // ---- step 4 ---------------------------------------------------------

    /** @param Collection<string, Role> $roles */
    private function stepAdopt(PermissionSyncResult $result, Collection $roles, bool $write): void
    {
        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
            $role = $roles->get($template->value);
            if ($role === null || $role->getAttribute('template_key') !== null) {
                continue;   // adoption is ONE-WAY and ONCE
            }

            /** @var list<string> $current */
            $current = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
            $pristine = $this->legacyBaseline->matchesVersion0($template->value, $current);

            $pristine ? $result->adoptedPristine++ : $result->adoptedCustomised++;
            $result->log[] = sprintf('adopt %s as %s', $template->value, $pristine ? 'pristine' : 'customised');

            if ($write) {
                DB::table('roles')->where('id', $role->getKey())->update([
                    'is_system' => true,
                    'template_key' => $template->value,
                    'template_version' => 0,
                    'customised_at' => $pristine ? null : $this->dates->now(),
                ]);
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- step 5 ---------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * @param  Collection<string, Role>  $roles
     * @return Collection<string, Role>
     */
    private function stepCreateMissingTemplateRoles(PermissionSyncResult $result, Collection $roles, string $tenantId, bool $write): Collection
    {
        $team = (string) config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key');
        $marked = $this->roleMarkers->tenantCarriesMarker(self::MARKER_SOURCE);

        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
            if ($roles->get($template->value) !== null) {
                continue;
            }

            $result->rolesCreated++;
            $result->log[] = 'create role '.$template->value;

            $attributes = [
                'name' => $template->value,
                'guard_name' => self::GUARD,
                $team => $tenantId,
                'is_system' => true,
                'template_key' => $template->value,
                'template_version' => $this->registry->version(),
                'customised_at' => null,
            ];

            // The ONE case in which provisioning_source is written, ever.
            if ($template === SystemRoleTemplate::GeneralManager && ! $marked) {
                $attributes['provisioning_source'] = self::MARKER_SOURCE;
                $result->markerWritten = 1;
                $result->log[] = 'marker written on general_manager';
            }

            if ($write) {
                $role = Role::create($attributes);
                $roles = $roles->put($template->value, $role);
            }
        }

        return $roles;
    }

    // ---- step 6 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepTemplateDeltas(PermissionSyncResult $result, string $tenantId, bool $write): void
    {
        $version = $this->registry->version();

        foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::declared() as $template) {   // non-admin only
            // LOCK AND RE-READ FIRST, in the lane's row order.
            $role = Role::query()
                ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
                ->where('template_key', $template->value)
                ->orderBy('name')
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->first();

            if ($role === null) {
                continue;
            }
            if ($role->getAttribute('customised_at') !== null) {
                $result->templatesSkippedCustomised++;   // a GAUGE
                $result->log[] = 'skip customised '.$template->value;

                continue;
            }

            if (! $write) {
                // Compute the delta without applying it: the applier writes.
                /** @var list<string> $current */
                $current = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
                $target = $this->registry->templateGrants()[$template->value] ?? [];
                $result->templatesApplied += count(array_diff($target, $current)) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
                $result->templatesRemoved += count(array_diff($current, $target)) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
                $result->templatesApplied += ((int) $role->getAttribute('template_version')) !== $version
                    && count(array_diff($target, $current)) === 0 ? 1 : 0;

                continue;
            }

            $delta = $this->applier->applyTo($role, $version);
            if ($delta->added !== []) {
                $result->templatesApplied++;
            }
            if ($delta->removed !== []) {
                $result->templatesRemoved++;
            }
            if ($delta->added === [] && $delta->removed === [] && $delta->versionChanged) {
                // A version-only advance is still a WRITE and must be counted,
                // or ALREADY_CURRENT would claim zero mutations over one row.
                $result->templatesApplied++;
            }
            if ($delta->added !== [] || $delta->removed !== []) {
                $result->log[] = sprintf('delta %s +%d -%d', $template->value, count($delta->added), count($delta->removed));
                $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                    roleId: (int) $role->getKey(),
                    roleName: (string) $role->getAttribute('name'),
                    templateKey: $template->value,
                    added: $delta->added,
                    removed: $delta->removed,
                    cause: 'template_delta',
                    fromTemplateVersion: $delta->fromVersion,
                    toTemplateVersion: $delta->toVersion,
                    tenantId: $tenantId,
                    actorUserId: null,
                    occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
                ));
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- step 7 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepDeprecationReplacements(PermissionSyncResult $result, string $tenantId, bool $write): void
    {
        foreach ($this->registry->definitions() as $definition) {
            if (! $definition->deprecated || $definition->replacedBy === null) {
                continue;   // all 22 of wave 1's deprecations are replacedBy: null
            }

            /** @var list<Role> $holders */
            $holders = Role::query()
                ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
                ->whereHas('permissions', static fn (QueryBuilder|\Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder $q) => $q->where('name', $definition->key))
                ->orderBy('name')
                ->orderBy('id')
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->get()
                ->all();

            foreach ($holders as $role) {
                /** @var list<string> $current */
                $current = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
                if (in_array($definition->replacedBy, $current, true)) {
                    continue;
                }

                $result->replacementsGranted++;
                $result->log[] = sprintf('replacement %s -> %s on %s', $definition->key, $definition->replacedBy, (string) $role->getAttribute('name'));

                if ($write) {
                    $role->givePermissionTo($definition->replacedBy);
                    $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                        roleId: (int) $role->getKey(),
                        roleName: (string) $role->getAttribute('name'),
                        templateKey: is_string($role->getAttribute('template_key')) ? (string) $role->getAttribute('template_key') : null,
                        added: [$definition->replacedBy],
                        removed: [],
                        cause: 'deprecation_replacement',
                        fromTemplateVersion: is_int($role->getAttribute('template_version')) ? (int) $role->getAttribute('template_version') : null,
                        toTemplateVersion: is_int($role->getAttribute('template_version')) ? (int) $role->getAttribute('template_version') : null,
                        tenantId: $tenantId,
                        actorUserId: null,
                        occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ---- step 8 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepAdmin(PermissionSyncResult $result, string $tenantId, bool $write): void
    {
        $admin = Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->where('template_key', SystemRoleTemplate::Admin->value)
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->first();

        if ($admin === null) {
            // Fail CLOSED. A tenant whose routes are gated on keys nobody holds
            // 403s every caller including the operator. (Wave 0b's AdminRoleMissing,
            // re-expressed as an outcome now that the sentinel class is deleted.)
            $result->failed('admin_role_missing');

            return;
        }

        $target = $this->registry->activeKeys();
        /** @var list<string> $current */
        $current = $admin->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
        $changes = count(array_diff($target, $current)) + count(array_diff($current, $target));

        if ($changes === 0) {
            return;
        }

        $result->adminChanges = $changes;   // INCLUDING removals
        $result->log[] = sprintf('admin +%d -%d', count(array_diff($target, $current)), count(array_diff($current, $target)));

        if ($write) {
            $admin->syncPermissions($target);
        }
    }

    // ---- step 9 ---------------------------------------------------------

    private function stepCacheAfterCommit(): void
    {
        // Registered INSIDE the transaction so it fires once the commit lands,
        // and while the permissions team id is still this tenant's — the S-1 key
        // scheme derives the cache namespace from it.
        DB::afterCommit(function (): void {
            $this->permissionRegistrar->forgetCachedPermissions();
        });
    }

    // ---- shared helpers -------------------------------------------------

    private function permissionExists(string $name): bool
    {
        return DB::table('permissions')->where('name', $name)->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)->exists();
    }

    /**
     * lane/w-lot-a-1a's resolveCatalogueRoles(), as wave 0b re-expressed it:
     * NULL-or-tenant team predicate, tenant_id PRESERVED as found, ordered name
     * then id, lockForUpdate, and both refusals.
     *
     * @return Collection<string, Role>
     */
    private function resolveTemplateRoles(string $tenantId): Collection
    {
        $team = (string) config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key');
        $names = array_map(static fn (SystemRoleTemplate $t): string => $t->value, SystemRoleTemplate::cases());

        /** @var Collection<int, Role> $roles */
        $roles = Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', self::GUARD)
            ->whereIn('name', $names)
            ->where(fn ($query) => $query->whereNull($team)->orWhere($team, $tenantId))
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->get();

        foreach ($roles->groupBy('name') as $name => $matches) {
            if ($name === SystemRoleTemplate::GeneralManager->value && $matches->contains(
                static fn (Role $role): bool => $role->getAttribute('provisioning_source') !== self::MARKER_SOURCE,
            )) {
                throw new DomainException('unmarked_general_manager_collision');
            }
            if ($matches->count() !== 1) {
                throw new DomainException('ambiguous_legacy_role_collision');
            }
        }

        return $roles->keyBy('name');
    }
}
~~~

### Step 3 — `permissions:sync`

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\PermissionSyncService;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

final class SyncPermissions extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:sync
        {--tenant= : Sync one tenant by id or slug; omit to sync the current tenant context}
        {--dry-run : Compute and report the plan; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Apply the permission catalogue and the additive template delta to one tenant.';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly PermissionSyncService $sync,
        private readonly PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        $selector = $this->option('tenant');
        $selector = is_string($selector) && $selector !== '' ? $selector : null;

        if ($selector !== null && ! (bool) config('tenancy_resolver.db_per_tenant')) {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=BLOCKED reason=compatibility_mode_is_global');
            $this->line('In compatibility mode the roles table is shared, so --tenant cannot mean anything. Run without it.');

            return 2;   // BLOCKED, on the applying AND the dry-run path
        }

        $tenantId = $selector !== null
            ? (string) (Tenant::query()->where('id', $selector)->orWhere('slug', $selector)->value('id') ?? '')
            : (string) ($this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId() ?? '');

        if ($tenantId === '') {
            $this->error('PERMISSIONS-SYNC outcome=FAILED reason=no_tenant_context');

            return 1;
        }

        $result = $this->sync->sync($tenantId, $write);
        $marker = $result->markerLine($tenantId, $write);

        $this->line($marker);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);
        foreach ($result->log as $line) {
            $this->line('  '.$line);
        }
        foreach ($result->staleRenames as $stale) {
            $this->warn('  stale rename entry (neither side present): '.$stale);
        }

        return $result->outcome->exitCode();
    }
}
~~~

### Step 4 — `permissions:sync-fleet`, on 0b's runner, unchanged

**Wave 1 passes it a different closure and a different marker prefix, and nothing more** — the universe algorithm, the selector contract, the readiness predicates, the failure isolation, the population gauge and the exit rule are **already** written and already tested. **`--force` does not exist and is not added.** A sync that is additive, template-delta and idempotent has nothing for a force flag to override, and W-LOT-A-1a's tenth guard rail is literally *"no force option exists"*.

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\PermissionSyncService;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\DTOs\FleetTenantOutcome;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\Services\TenantFleetRunner;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Enums\FleetOutcome;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

final class SyncPermissionsFleet extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:sync-fleet
        {--tenants=* : limit to these tenant ids or slugs; omit for every tenant}
        {--dry-run   : compute and report every tenant plan; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Apply the permission catalogue and the template delta across the tenant fleet.';

    private const MARKER_PREFIX = 'PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly TenantFleetRunner $runner,
        private readonly PermissionSyncService $sync,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        /** @var list<string> $selectors */
        $selectors = $this->option('tenants');

        $result = $this->runner->run($selectors, self::MARKER_PREFIX, function (string $tenantId) use ($write): FleetTenantOutcome {
            $syncResult = $this->sync->sync($tenantId, $write);
            $marker = $syncResult->markerLine($tenantId, $write);
            $this->line($marker);
            Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

            return match ($syncResult->outcome->value) {
                'APPLIED' => FleetTenantOutcome::applied($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                'ALREADY_CURRENT' => FleetTenantOutcome::alreadyCurrent($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                'BLOCKED' => FleetTenantOutcome::blocked($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
                default => FleetTenantOutcome::failed($tenantId, $syncResult->reason),
            };
        });

        // The COMMAND renders the aggregate line and owns the exit code — never the runner.
        $aggregate = sprintf(
            '%s mode=%s tenants=%d applied=%d already_current=%d blocked=%d failed=%d provisioned_during_run=%d',
            self::MARKER_PREFIX,
            $write ? 'APPLY' : 'DRY_RUN',
            $result->tenants(),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Applied),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::AlreadyCurrent),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Blocked),
            $result->count(FleetOutcome::Failed),
            $result->provisionedDuringRun,
        );
        $this->line($aggregate);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($aggregate);

        foreach ($result->outcomes as $outcome) {
            if (! $outcome->outcome->isGreen()) {
                $this->error(sprintf('  %s %s reason=%s', $outcome->outcome->value, $outcome->subject, $outcome->reason));
            }
        }

        if ($result->count(FleetOutcome::Failed) > 0) {
            return 1;
        }

        return $result->count(FleetOutcome::Blocked) > 0 ? 2 : 0;
    }
}
~~~

### Step 5 — the deletions, and the ONE test they force to change shape

```
git rm apps/api/app/Console/Commands/EnsurePermissions.php \
       apps/api/app/Console/Commands/EnsurePermissionsFleet.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/EnsurePermissionsRunner.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/DryRunRollback.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/AdminRoleMissing.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Application/DTOs/EnsurePermissionsResult.php \
       apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/Domain/Enums/EnsurePermissionsOutcome.php \
       scripts/permissions-ensure-0b.sh \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsCustomisationGuardTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsIdempotencyTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Tenant/EnsurePermissionsFleetTest.php
git mv apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/EnsurePermissionsRaceTest.php \
       apps/api/tests/Feature/Identity/PermissionWriteLockConcurrentWriterTest.php
```

> **`PermissionsSyncSupersedesEnsureTest` cannot INVOKE `permissions:ensure`, because this commit deletes it.** It therefore **constructs the state `ensure` leaves** and syncs on top of it — which is a strictly better test, because it asserts against the *end state* the 0b deploy actually produced rather than against a command's behaviour. The fixture builder is:
>
> ```php
> /** The tenant state an ensure-provisioned tenant is in: every catalogue key in its
>  *  PRE-rename spelling, and every seeded template holding exactly version0(). */
> private function seedEnsureProvisionedTenant(string $tenantId): void
> {
>     // 325 permission rows: the 306 lane keys in their PRE-rename spelling
>     // (eleven of them are catalog_cart.* / pos_held_orders.* / *.edit) plus
>     // wave 0b's nineteen, which `ensure` created.
>     foreach (LegacyRoleBaselineFixture::preRenameCatalogue() as $key) {
>         Permission::create(['name' => $key, 'guard_name' => 'sanctum']);
>     }
>     foreach (SystemRoleTemplate::cases() as $template) {
>         $role = Role::create(['name' => $template->value, 'guard_name' => 'sanctum', $team => $tenantId]);
>         $role->syncPermissions(LegacyRoleBaseline::version0($template->value));
>     }
> }
> ```
>
> | Run | Expected |
> |---|---|
> | first `permissions:sync` on that tenant | `outcome=APPLIED`; **`created=0`** — the mechanical proof that `ensure` left nothing for sync to create; **`renamed=11`** — the full rename map, because `ensure` **never renames** and every source is present with no target present; `adopted_pristine + adopted_customised` = 8, the number of template-named roles; **`adopted_customised=0`**, because the eleven renames are absorbed by `canonical()` on both sides and `ensure`'s equality-guarded grants are **inside** `version0` by construction; `templates_applied` counts only the version-0 → version-1 advance; `templates_removed` counts the templates losing a deprecated key; `marker_written` is 0 or 1 by whether the tenant was marked |
> | second `permissions:sync` | `ALREADY_CURRENT`, every `mutations` counter zero, **zero database writes** by query-log count; the two gauges asserted **unchanged from the first run**, not zero |
>
> `created=0` is the real superseding claim. `renamed=11` is the second half a gate should check: it is the number that proves the two commands have **disjoint jobs** rather than overlapping ones. Expecting `renamed=0` would ship a red test.

### Step 6 — the tests

- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::a_second_run_writes_no_row` — **zero write statements by query-log count** plus every `mutations` counter zero, and deliberately asserting **nothing** about the two gauges. **This is the B1-3 red at the service level**: with rev 1's unconditional `template_version` update it fails with seven writes.
- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::sync_creates_every_registry_permission_in_a_fresh_tenant_and_in_a_second_tenant` — **two physical tenant databases**, asserting data meaning; tenant B's customised `manager` is skipped independently of tenant A's.
- [ ] `PermissionsSyncTest::the_cache_is_flushed_after_commit_not_before` — asserted by observing a concurrent read on a **second connection**.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_renamed_pristine_role_adopts_as_pristine`.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncAdoptionTest::a_role_holding_exactly_the_0b_additions_adopts_as_pristine`.
- [ ] **EC-9c** — `a_legacy_never_delta_d_tenant_adopts_manager_as_customised`: a tenant on which `LOT_ACTION_PERMISSIONS_ENFORCE` stayed `false` and which was never marked holds seven roles from `createLegacyRoles()`, with `manager` still holding `batches.recall` and lacking `batches.recall.request`. That is a real, **non-normalisable** difference from v0, so `manager` adopts **customised** and the other six adopt pristine; step 5 then creates `general_manager` and writes the marker (`roles_created=1`, `marker_written=1`).
- [ ] `PermissionSyncMarkerTest` — the **five negatives** (never cleared, never changed, never widened to another role, never widened to another source, never written on a role sync did not create in the same statement) and the **one positive**.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncServiceTest::a_marked_tenant_sees_no_provisioning_source_write` — asserted **by query log**.
- [ ] `PermissionSyncServiceTest::a_missing_admin_role_fails_closed` — `outcome=FAILED`, `reason=admin_role_missing`, **exit 1 on the applying AND the dry-run path**, and **zero rows written**. This is 0b's `AdminRoleMissing` behaviour, preserved through the sentinel's deletion.

## Task 1.11: the two triggers — provisioning, the entrypoint, and the health check

**Exactly two sync triggers, and a rolling migration is neither.**

1. **Provisioning.** `TenantInitializationService::seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing()` (`dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenantInitializationService.php:199-217`, called at `dev:…:75`) is **replaced** by a constructor-injected `PermissionSyncService::sync($tenantId, write: true)` — no `Artisan::call('db:seed')`, no *"if admin exists"* guard, because sync is idempotent and needs no guard. The call **keeps its position at `:75`**, i.e. before `assignDefaultRoles()` at `dev:…:78`, so the role exists when it is assigned.

   **Marker ownership, stated once so the three contracts agree:** `PermissionSyncService::sync()` **returns** a result and **emits no marker** — marker rendering belongs to the *caller*, which is why the marker line lives on the command and why the fleet command, not the service, renders the aggregate line. Provisioning does not go through the command, so **`TenantInitializationService` renders the returned result** into the `PERMISSIONS-SYNC` line (`mode=APPLY`, on the log channel **and** the durable stderr channel), exactly once per provisioning. This adds **no third trigger** — it is trigger 1 reporting the call it already makes.

   ~~~php
   // TenantInitializationService — constructor gains one dependency
   public function __construct(
       // (every existing dependency, unchanged)
       private readonly PermissionSyncService $permissionSync,
   ) {}

   /**
    * Replaces seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing(). Sync is idempotent, so the
    * "does admin already exist" guard is gone: a guard that skips the whole
    * catalogue because one role happens to exist is exactly the shape of I-20.
    */
   private function syncPermissionsForTenant(Tenant $tenant): void
   {
       $result = $this->permissionSync->sync((string) $tenant->id, write: true);
       $marker = $result->markerLine((string) $tenant->id, true);
       Log::info($marker);
       Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);
   }
   ~~~
   and at `:75`, `$this->seedRolesAndPermissionsIfMissing();` becomes `$this->syncPermissionsForTenant($tenant);`. The private method, the `Artisan` import and the `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder` import are **deleted, not re-pointed** — that is reference 7 of Task 1.14's census.

2. **Deploy.** The entrypoint block under `SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT`, and nothing else.

**There is NO `DatabaseMigrated` listener.** `tenants:migrate` dispatches that event once per tenant on **every** invocation including a no-op one, and `RollingTenantMigrationCommand` calls `tenants:migrate` once per tenant from an entrypoint line that is **unconditional** (`dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:150`) and runs **before** the flagged block. With a listener, the flag `false` would still sync every rolling-migrated tenant and `true` would sync every tenant **twice**, and neither listener run would be represented by the status file — OQ-4 would then be a question about a flag that does not govern the behaviour it names.

### The entrypoint block

`dev:apps/api/docker/entrypoint.sh:156-170` — the comment block plus the flagged `tenants:seed` call — is replaced. The surrounding lines are **not**: `:150-154` runs `tenants:migrate-rolling --force` **unconditionally** and catches its failure, which is exactly why a not-ready tenant must be a fleet **failure** rather than a skip.

The `mkdir` goes beside the existing runtime-directory block at `dev:…:19-23`, **not** inside the flagged block, so the directory exists even on a boot that never runs a sync and `HealthCheckService` never reads a path whose parent is missing.

~~~sh
# --- beside the existing `mkdir -p /var/run` at entrypoint.sh:19-23 -------
mkdir -p /var/run/autoerp
chmod 755 /var/run/autoerp
~~~

~~~sh
# --- replacing entrypoint.sh:156-170 -------------------------------------
    # Apply the code-owned permission catalogue and the additive template delta
    # to every tenant database. OPT-IN (SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT=true) while the
    # one-week staging soak runs; OQ-4 decides the production default from the
    # soak's evidence.
    #
    # This REPLACES the destructive `tenants:seed RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`
    # that ran here before (I-20): sync is additive, applies an EXACT diff only
    # to uncustomised template-linked roles, and touches a custom or customised
    # role in exactly one audited case.
    if [ "$SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT" = "true" ]; then
        echo ""
        echo "Syncing the permission catalogue across tenant databases (direct -> $DIRECT_DB_HOST)..."
        if DB_HOST="$DIRECT_DB_HOST" php artisan permissions:sync-fleet; then
            echo "  Permission sync: [all tenants applied or already current]"
            # SUCCESS OVERWRITES the file. `>` truncates, so a recovered deploy
            # clears a previous boot's failure marker; writing only the failure
            # arm would leave a container that recovered permanently unhealthy.
            printf '%s\n' "permissions_sync=ok" > /var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status
        else
            echo "  Permission sync: [FAILED - see the PERMISSIONS-SYNC-FLEET marker above]"
            printf '%s\n' "permissions_sync=failed" > /var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status
        fi
    fi
~~~

**The entrypoint keeps booting** — refusing to start the container on a permission-sync failure would turn a partial authorization problem into a total outage — **but it stops lying about it.**

### The reader

`HealthCheckService` gains a fifth check in the array at `dev:apps/api/app/Modules/Admin/Application/Services/HealthCheckService.php:19-24`. Because `check()` derives `$allHealthy` from **every** check at `:26`, a failed sync also flips the aggregate — which is the point.

~~~php
    private const SYNC_STATUS_FILE = '/var/run/autoerp/permissions-sync.status';

    /**
     * Read the boot sync's status file. THREE states, and "absent" is healthy:
     * a container that never ran a boot sync (SYNC_PERMISSIONS_ON_BOOT unset,
     * which is the production default during the soak) is not unhealthy.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    private function checkPermissionsSync(): array
    {
        if (! is_file(self::SYNC_STATUS_FILE)) {
            return ['healthy' => true, 'state' => 'not_run'];
        }

        $contents = trim((string) @file_get_contents(self::SYNC_STATUS_FILE));

        if ($contents === 'permissions_sync=failed') {
            return ['healthy' => false, 'error' => 'permission sync reported failure on last boot'];
        }

        return ['healthy' => $contents === 'permissions_sync=ok', 'state' => $contents];
    }
~~~
and in `check()`'s array: `'permissions_sync' => $this->checkPermissionsSync(),`.

**The key is exposed on the AUTHENTICATED `/api/v1/admin/monitoring/health`** (`apps/api/routes/api.php:83`, inside `['auth:sanctum-admin', 'super_admin', 'throttle:admin-sensitive']`). **The public `/api/v1/health` is unchanged** and stays a two-field liveness probe for the load balancer: a public endpoint must not disclose the platform's internal authorization state, and flipping it to 503 on a permission-sync failure would take the whole fleet out of the load balancer over a *partial* authorization problem — the opposite of "boot continues".

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the tests first**, then the three edits.
- [ ] **Step 2 — `TenantProvisioningSyncTest`** — a freshly provisioned tenant holds `registry->activeKeys()` on `admin`, **with no boot flag set and no migration event involved**.
- [ ] **Step 3 — EC-40** — a completed provisioning emits **exactly one** `PERMISSIONS-SYNC tenant=<uuid> mode=APPLY outcome=APPLIED` line, and a provisioning that never reached its sync emits **none**.
- [ ] **Step 4 — `PermissionSyncTriggerCensusTest`** — a static-analysis assertion that **no** production listener, subscriber or service-provider binding references `Stancl\Tenancy\Events\DatabaseMigrated`, and that the only production callers of `PermissionSyncService::sync()` are `TenantInitializationService`, `SyncPermissions`, `SyncPermissionsFleet` and (from Task 1.14) `RolesAndPermissionsSeeder`.
- [ ] **Step 5 — `EntrypointPermissionSyncShapeTest`** — a shell-source-shape test over `docker/entrypoint.sh`: the status file is written on **both** arms, with `>` (never `>>`), `mkdir -p /var/run/autoerp` appears **outside** the flagged block, the flagged block calls `permissions:sync-fleet` and **never** `tenants:seed`, and no `exit` follows either arm.
- [ ] **Step 6 — `HealthCheckPermissionSyncTest`** — absent file ⇒ healthy; `=ok` ⇒ healthy; `=failed` ⇒ unhealthy **and the aggregate flips**; **EC-32d**, a successful boot **clears** a failed boot's health state (write `failed`, then `ok`, assert healthy); and the **public** `/v1/health` body is byte-identical to today's.

## Task 1.12: the `customised_at` writer and *Re-apply template*

**Why the writer cannot wait for wave 2.** Wave 1 already deploys and repeatedly runs sync, so during the soak the existing editor would keep calling `syncPermissions()` **without marking customisation**, and the next sync could add template grants to a role the tenant had just edited. The column and the writer that sets it **must not ship a wave apart**.

**The writer's transaction is the SAME transaction as its `syncPermissions()`, and it takes the shared lock.** Today `RoleController::update` resolves the role at `lane/w-lot-a-1a:…/RoleController.php:239` and later calls `syncPermissions()` at `:265` with **no transaction and no lock at all** — 0b-14 wraps it; wave 1 adds the flag write inside that same wrapper. The operator edit becomes, in one transaction: `PermissionWriteLock::acquire($tenantId)` → `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the role (ordered `roles.name`, `roles.id`) → compute the audit diff → `syncPermissions()` → `customised_at = now()` → commit. The flag and the grant set it describes are then written **or rolled back together**, which is what lets step 6's post-lock re-read be trusted: there is no window in which the grants have changed and the flag has not.

**Set `customised_at = now()` only when a system role's permission set ACTUALLY CHANGES and `customised_at IS NULL`.** A no-op save must not mark a role customised — that would withhold every future delta from a tenant that changed nothing.

~~~php
// RoleController::update — the permissions branch, replacing lane :263-266
if (isset($validated['permissions'])) {
    /** @var list<string> $requested */
    $requested = array_values($validated['permissions']);
    $tenantId = (string) $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();

    DB::transaction(function () use ($role, $requested, $tenantId): void {
        // (i) the lock is the FIRST statement — wave 0b's helper, unchanged.
        $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

        // (ii) re-read the role UNDER the lock, in the lane's row order.
        /** @var Role $locked */
        $locked = Role::query()
            ->whereKey($role->getKey())
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->firstOrFail();

        /** @var list<string> $before */
        $before = $locked->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();
        $added = array_values(array_diff($requested, $before));
        $removed = array_values(array_diff($before, $requested));

        // (iii) the grant write.
        $locked->syncPermissions($requested);

        // (iv) the flag, in the SAME transaction. Only on a REAL change, only
        // on a system role, and only once — it records the FIRST edit.
        if (($added !== [] || $removed !== [])
            && (bool) $locked->getAttribute('is_system')
            && $locked->getAttribute('customised_at') === null) {
            DB::table('roles')->where('id', $locked->getKey())->update(['customised_at' => $this->dates->now()]);
        }
    });
}
~~~

***Re-apply template* is a `RoleController` action over one role, not a sync mode.**

~~~php
/**
 * POST /api/v1/roles/{id}/reapply-template
 *
 * Restores one system role to its template. The five obligations, in order and
 * in ONE transaction: refuse unless template-linked; clear customised_at INSIDE
 * the lock so a concurrent sync cannot observe the half-state; apply the exact
 * diff through the ONE applier; advance the version; emit RoleSyncedV1 with a
 * NON-NULL actor, which is the only difference from a sync-emitted event.
 */
#[CrossTenantRoute(reason: 'Spatie TeamScope auto-scoping: Role::findOrFail($id) is filtered by team_id; a role from another team 404s here.')]
public function reapplyTemplate(Request $request, int $id): JsonResponse
{
    $role = Role::findOrFail($id);

    if (! (bool) $role->getAttribute('is_system') || ! is_string($role->getAttribute('template_key'))) {
        return response()->json([
            'error' => ['code' => 'ROLE_HAS_NO_TEMPLATE', 'message' => 'Only a system role linked to a template can be re-applied.'],
        ], 422);
    }

    $tenantId = (string) $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
    $actorId = $request->user()?->getAuthIdentifier();
    $version = $this->registry->version();

    $delta = DB::transaction(function () use ($role, $tenantId, $version, $actorId): TemplateDelta {
        $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

        /** @var Role $locked */
        $locked = Role::query()->whereKey($role->getKey())->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')->lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail();

        // (b) clear the flag INSIDE the lock, before the apply.
        DB::table('roles')->where('id', $locked->getKey())->update(['customised_at' => null]);
        $locked->refresh();

        $delta = $this->applier->applyTo($locked, $version);

        if ($delta->added !== [] || $delta->removed !== []) {
            $this->events->dispatch(new RoleSyncedV1(
                roleId: (int) $locked->getKey(),
                roleName: (string) $locked->getAttribute('name'),
                templateKey: (string) $locked->getAttribute('template_key'),
                added: $delta->added,
                removed: $delta->removed,
                cause: 'template_reapply',
                fromTemplateVersion: $delta->fromVersion,
                toTemplateVersion: $delta->toVersion,
                tenantId: $tenantId,
                actorUserId: $actorId === null ? null : (string) $actorId,
                occurredAtIso: $this->dates->now()->toIso8601String(),
            ));
        }

        return $delta;
    });

    $role->refresh()->load('permissions');

    return response()->json([
        'data' => [
            'role' => (array) $this->roleData($role),
            'added' => $delta->added,
            'removed' => $delta->removed,
            'from_template_version' => $delta->fromVersion,
            'to_template_version' => $delta->toVersion,
        ],
        'meta' => [
            'timestamp' => now()->toIso8601String(),
            'request_id' => $request->header('X-Request-ID', (string) uuid_create()),
        ],
    ]);
}
~~~

and the route, beside the five existing role routes in `apps/api/app/Modules/Identity/routes.php` (the group at `lane/w-lot-a-1a:…:57-64`, which already carries `['api', 'auth:sanctum', SetPermissionsTeam::class, EnforceTokenTenantClaim::class]`):
```php
    Route::post('roles/{id}/reapply-template', [RoleController::class, 'reapplyTemplate'])
        ->middleware('can:roles.manage')
        ->name('roles.reapply-template');
```
> `roles.manage` is an **existing** catalogue key held by `admin` only, so this route adds no key, does not move either route-coverage ceiling in the *uncovered* direction, and needs no baseline edit — it is a **new** route and it ships **gated**, which is what the ratchet's anti-growth direction requires.

`RoleController`'s constructor gains four dependencies — `PermissionWriteLock $writeLock`, `TemplateDeltaApplier $applier`, `PermissionRegistry $registry`, `Dispatcher $events`, `DateFactory $dates` — all `private readonly`, none through `app()`.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — the tests first**, then the controller changes and the route.
- [ ] **Step 2 — `PermissionWriteLockRaceTest` (EC-20a)**, two PG connections: operator edit versus sync. **The outcome contract, which is what a gate checks rather than the mechanism:** whichever order the two transactions serialize in, if the operator commits first, sync's post-lock re-read sees `customised_at IS NOT NULL` and **skips** the role into the `templates_skipped_customised` gauge; if sync commits first, it applies its delta to a then-pristine role and the operator's edit lands afterwards, setting `customised_at` on top. **There is no interleaving in which a write is lost, and none in which a role is left `customised_at IS NOT NULL` carrying a grant set the operator did not choose.**
- [ ] **Step 3 — `CustomisedAtWriterTest`**: a no-op save does **not** set the flag; a real change does; a second change does **not** move it (it records the **first** edit); a **custom** role is unaffected (it has no template to be customised against); and the flag and the grants are written in **one** transaction (asserted by rolling back and observing neither).
- [ ] **Step 4 — `TemplateReapplyTest`**: refuses (422, `ROLE_HAS_NO_TEMPLATE`) on a custom role and on a system role with no `template_key`; clears the flag, applies the exact diff, advances the version and emits `RoleSyncedV1` with a **non-null** actor; a re-apply on an **already pristine** role at the current version is a visible **no-op** (empty diff, no event, **zero writes by query-log count** — the conditional `template_version` write again); and `403` for a caller without `roles.manage`.
- [ ] **Step 5 — extend `PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest`** using 0b rev 2's three-way split:
      - `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS` gains `app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/PermissionSyncService.php::sync`, `app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::update` and `…::reapplyTemplate`;
      - **`LOCK_INHERITED_FROM` gains** `app/Modules/Identity/Domain/Authorization/TemplateDeltaApplier.php::applyTo` ⇒ `[PermissionSyncService::sync, RoleController::reapplyTemplate]`;
      - **`INITIALIZATION_ONLY` must not grow.**
- [ ] **Step 6 — run, analyse, format:**
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w1/apps/api
      DB_CONNECTION=pgsql DB_HOST=127.0.0.1 DB_PORT=5433 DB_DATABASE=autoerp_test_w1 DB_CENTRAL_DATABASE=autoerp_test_w1_central \
        php artisan test -c phpunit-pgsql.xml tests/Feature/Identity/PermissionWriteLockRaceTest.php \
                                              tests/Feature/Identity/CustomisedAtWriterTest.php \
                                              tests/Feature/Identity/TemplateReapplyTest.php \
                                              tests/Feature/Identity/PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest.php \
                                              tests/Feature/Identity/TemplateDeltaApplierParityTest.php
      ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --level=8 app/Modules/Identity/Presentation
      ./vendor/bin/pint --test app/Modules/Identity/Presentation tests/Feature/Identity
      ```
- [ ] **Step 7 — stage and commit:**

## Task 1.13: separation of duties

> **SoD-1.** For every `sod_group` in the registry, no **non-`admin`** system-role template may hold both a `create`-class key and a **financially decisive** key (`post`, `reverse`, `void`, `approve`, `confirm`, `refund`, `allocate`, `pay`) from that group. A `legacy()` key participates through its declared `behavesAs`.

**Three scoping decisions, each stated so a gate can challenge them.** (1) **Templates, not roles** — the rule constrains what the platform *ships*; a tenant may still create a custom role holding both, because a five-person pharmacy legitimately has one person who raises and approves POs, and none of Odoo, ERPNext or Dolibarr implements NIST SSD/DSD generically. (2) **`admin` is exempt**, because `admin` holds everything by definition. (3) **Exactly eight groups, and the list is closed for wave 1**: `purchase-order`, `supplier-invoice`, `invoice`, `credit-note`, `payment`, `expense`, `journal`, `stock-adjustment`.

**The group memberships, mechanically, as Task 1.4's manifests declare them** — twenty keys, and `purchase-orders.approve` is **not** among them because it does not exist:

| `sod_group` | Member keys |
|---|---|
| `purchase-order` | `purchase-orders.create`, `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| `supplier-invoice` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Create`)*, `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Approve`)* |
| `invoice` | `invoices.create`, `invoices.post` |
| `credit-note` | `credit-notes.create`, `credit-notes.post` |
| `payment` | `payments.create`, `payments.pay-supplier` *(legacy, `behavesAs: Pay`)*, `payments.allocate`, `payments.reverse`, `payments.refund` |
| `expense` | `expenses.create`, `expenses.post`, `expenses.pay` |
| `journal` | `journal.create`, `journal.post` |
| `stock-adjustment` | `inventory.adjustments.create`, `inventory.adjustments.post` |

`payments.reverse` is a member even though **no template holds it** — it is admin-only by an earlier owner decision — so it adds no baseline row while making a future grant of it to `manager` an immediate failure. That is the hard half doing its job before the fact.

**`order`, `delivery`, `income` and `work-order` are deliberately NOT SoD groups**, even though `manager` holds create-plus-decisive combinations in all four. They are **operational** transitions, not financially decisive ones: a sales-order confirmation, a delivery confirmation, an income posting against an already-recorded receipt and a work-order approval each commit the business to *doing work*, not to *money changing hands or a fiscal fact becoming real*. All three reference systems draw the same line. **The cost is stated honestly:** a manager who can raise and confirm a sales order can commit stock without a second pair of eyes. That is an accepted residual — the compensating control is the fiscal chain and the stock-movement audit — and re-opening it means **adding a group**, which the hard half makes a deliberate, reviewed act rather than a drift.

**The test ships in two halves.** **Hard half:** any `sod_group` introduced from wave 1 onward, and any template grant **added** to an existing group, fails immediately. **No baseline entry may be added, ever.** **Ratchet half:** the existing combinations are baselined **by name** in `permission-sod-baseline.json`, counted against a shrink-only `SOD_BASELINE_CEILING = 18`.

**The baseline is EIGHTEEN, re-derived from the manifests of Task 1.4** (eleven on the pre-lane tree; `general_manager`'s grant set is `manager`'s plus two keys, so it inherits the seven `manager` rows verbatim — **none of them a new decision**):

| # | `sod_group` | Template | Create-class key | Decisive key(s) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `journal` | `accountant` | `journal.create` | `journal.post` |
| 2 | `credit-note` | `manager` | `credit-notes.create` | `credit-notes.post` |
| 3 | `credit-note` | `general_manager` | `credit-notes.create` | `credit-notes.post` |
| 4 | `invoice` | `manager` | `invoices.create` | `invoices.post` |
| 5 | `invoice` | `general_manager` | `invoices.create` | `invoices.post` |
| 6 | `expense` | `manager` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 7 | `expense` | `general_manager` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 8 | `expense` | `accountant` | `expenses.create` | `expenses.pay`, `expenses.post` |
| 9 | `stock-adjustment` | `manager` | `inventory.adjustments.create` | `inventory.adjustments.post` |
| 10 | `stock-adjustment` | `general_manager` | `inventory.adjustments.create` | `inventory.adjustments.post` |
| 11 | `purchase-order` | `manager` | `purchase-orders.create` | `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| 12 | `purchase-order` | `general_manager` | `purchase-orders.create` | `purchase-orders.confirm` |
| 13 | `supplier-invoice` | `manager` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 14 | `supplier-invoice` | `general_manager` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 15 | `supplier-invoice` | `accountant` | `supplier-invoices.create-pending` | `supplier-invoices.approve-invoice-first` |
| 16 | `payment` | `manager` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |
| 17 | `payment` | `general_manager` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |
| 18 | `payment` | `accountant` | `payments.create` | `payments.allocate`, `payments.pay-supplier`, `payments.refund` |

**Four of the eight shipped templates satisfy the rule with no change** — `cashier` holds `invoices.create`, `expenses.create` and `payments.create` but no decisive key in any group; `operator` is likewise create-and-update only; `viewer` and `technician` hold no create-class key in any of the eight groups. **That is the evidence it is a real rule rather than a rule nothing can pass**, and it is verified against the post-0b grant map rather than asserted.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — check O-5/OQ-3 first.** If the owner has ruled, apply the retirement to the manifests' `templateDefaults` (Task 1.4c's `Document` manifest for the invoice/credit-note rows, Task 1.4g's `Treasury` manifest for the payment row) **before** generating the baseline, and pin the ceiling at 15 or 12. If not, ship eighteen. **Do not decide it here.**
- [ ] **Step 2 — generate `permission-sod-baseline.json` from the registry** and assert the generated set **equals** the table above (or the ruled-down table). The ceiling is the count, and it only falls.
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w1/apps/api && php artisan tinker --execute='
        $r = app(\App\Shared\Domain\Authorization\PermissionRegistry::class);
        $grants = $r->templateGrants(); unset($grants["admin"]);
        $byKey = []; foreach ($r->definitions() as $d) { $byKey[$d->key] = $d; }
        $rows = [];
        foreach ($r->sodGroups() as $group => $keys) {
          foreach ($grants as $template => $held) {
            $c = array_values(array_filter($keys, fn($k) => in_array($k,$held,true) && $byKey[$k]->isCreateClass()));
            $d = array_values(array_filter($keys, fn($k) => in_array($k,$held,true) && $byKey[$k]->isFinanciallyDecisive()));
            if ($c && $d) { sort($c); sort($d); $rows[] = ["sod_group"=>$group,"template"=>$template,"create"=>$c,"decisive"=>$d]; }
          }
        }
        usort($rows, fn($a,$b) => [$a["sod_group"],$a["template"]] <=> [$b["sod_group"],$b["template"]]);
        file_put_contents("tests/Architecture/baselines/permission-sod-baseline.json", json_encode($rows, JSON_PRETTY_PRINT|JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES|JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE)."\n");
        echo count($rows), PHP_EOL;'
      ```
      **Expected: 18.**
- [ ] **Step 3 — `PermissionSodTemplateTest`**, a **pure registry test** — no database, no fixtures, milliseconds on both lanes. It reports violations as `sod_group 'purchase-order': template 'manager' holds both purchase-orders.create and purchase-orders.confirm`. Three cases: `no_unbaselined_combination_exists` (the hard half), `the_baseline_is_within_the_shrink_only_ceiling`, and `four_templates_are_clean` (naming `cashier`, `operator`, `viewer`, `technician` — the evidence case).
- [ ] **Step 4 — the hard half's own red:** add a throw-away definition giving `general_manager` a decisive key inside a group where it already holds a create-class key — the cheapest is `PermissionDefinition::make('Accounting', 'journal', PermissionVerb::Post, templateDefaults: ['general_manager'], sodGroup: 'journal')` alongside a `journal.create` grant to the same template — confirm the test fails naming **both** keys and the template, then remove it. Paste the failure into the handback.
- [ ] **Step 5 — run, analyse, format, stage, commit:**
      ```
      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w1/apps/api
      php artisan test tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionSodTemplateTest.php
      ./vendor/bin/pint --test tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionSodTemplateTest.php
      git add apps/api/tests/Feature/Authorization/PermissionSodTemplateTest.php \
              apps/api/tests/Architecture/baselines/permission-sod-baseline.json
      ```
